I'm the Singing Sword!\"", "@The_weapon@ chants, \"I am golden and pointed, and with blood well anointed.\"", "@The_weapon@ dictates a lengthy tome entitled 'The Well-Tempered Blade'.", "@The_weapon@ says, \"Whips just don't cut it for me, don't you agree?\"", "@The_weapon@ chants, \"Rather stab than club, rather slice than rub!\"", "@The_weapon@ states, \"Music critics are all talk and no body armour.\"", "@The_weapon@ demands, \"I must not be sheathed without first drawing blood!\"", "@The_weapon@ [explains all about|gives a lengthy lecture on] @_musical_topic_@.", "@The_weapon@ curses its smith, \"I was supposed to be the Stinging Sword!\"", "@The_weapon@ yells, \"Ploughshares to swords!\"", "@The_weapon@ asks, \"What song would you like to be played for your funeral?\"", "@The_weapon@ announces, \"I dedicate these songs to @player_name@!\"", "@The_weapon@ declares, \"I sing the tale of @player_name@, the warrior!\"", "@The_weapon@ says, \"Hey! Are you even listening?\"", "@The_weapon@ longs back for the days in the arena.", "@The_weapon@ amuses itself with detailed descriptions of past executions.", "@The_weapon@ wishes everyone in the dungeon [an unpleasant|a sudden] [demise|death].", "@The_weapon@ fumes, \"Being battered, bent and broken sure is better than this boredom!\"", "@The_weapon@ shouts, \"I love putting a song in people's hearts. And in their kidneys.\"", "@The_weapon@ grouses, \"Could you please kill something? I'm itching all over.\"", "@The_weapon@ laments, \"What are you waiting for? I am doing all the work.\"", "@The_weapon@ explains, \"Killing is an art, not a pastime.\"", "@The_weapon@ goes, \"Jessica and Sigmund, sitting in a tree...\"", "@The_weapon@ says, \"I want to meet the serpentine blade!\"", "@The_weapon@ says, \"Take me to Pandemonium! Now!!!\"", "@The_weapon@ recounts funny stories of hydra head multiplication.", "@The_weapon@ says, \"Sometimes people call me a magnificent bastard.\"", "@The_weapon@ snarls, \"Dear diary, yet another boring day with @player_name@...\"", "@The_weapon@ says, \"In my experience, @player_genus_plural@ are mediocre at best.\"", "@The_weapon@ groans, \"Among all @player_genus_plural@, @player_name@ is the worst.\"", "@The_weapon@ says, \"Unhand me, foul miscreant!\"", "@The_weapon@ muses, \"Whom do I have to kill to get some service around here?\"", "@The_weapon@ sings out, \"Sing loudly and carry a big sword!\"", "@The_weapon@ ponders the difference in lifespan between man and sword.", "@The_weapon@ says, \"All work and no play makes @player_name@ a dull boy!\"", "@The_weapon@ says, \"I wish I'd pick another to be my killing [tool|fool].\"", "@The_weapon@ hoarsely says, \"Need a drink... quick! Stab something.\"", "@The_weapon@ says, \"You haven't happened to see my sheath laying around anywhere? I feel so exposed.\"", "@The_weapon@ complains, \"Oh, are your palms wet and greasy. "@The_weapon@说:“我根本不适合用来鞭打，你同意吗？”", "@The_weapon@吟唱道：“宁刺不砍，宁切不擦！”", "@The_weapon@陈述：“音乐评论家都是在空谈，不过是纸上谈兵罢了。”", "@The_weapon@要求：“还没有让敌人流血，就不要把我收回剑鞘！”", "@The_weapon@就@_musical_topic_@这个话题进行了[详细阐述|长篇大论]。", "@The_weapon@诅咒着锻造它的铁匠：“我本应是那把刺叮剑！”", "@The_weapon@喊道：“铸犁为剑！”", "@The_weapon@问：“你想在葬礼上演奏哪首歌？”", "@The_weapon@宣布：“我将这些歌献给@player_name@！”", "@The_weapon@宣告：“我要歌颂勇士@player_name@的故事！”", "@The_weapon@说：“嘿！你在听吗？”", "@The_weapon@渴望回到在竞技场的日子。", "@The_weapon@叙说着过去的行刑细节以自娱自乐。", "@The_weapon@希望地牢中的每个人都有[一个不愉快的|一个突如其来的][败亡|死法]。", "@The_weapon@怒气冲冲地说：“即使被打扁、被打弯、被打碎也绝对比现在百无聊赖要强！”", "@The_weapon@叫道：“我喜欢把歌曲放在人们的心里。还有他们的肾脏里。”", "@The_weapon@发着牢骚：“你能杀些什么吗？我浑身发痒。”", "@The_weapon@悲叹道：“你在等什么？我在做所有工作。”", "@The_weapon@解释道：“杀戮是一门艺术，而不是消遣。”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“杰西卡和西格蒙德，坐在树上……”", "@The_weapon@说:“我想见见蛇刃！”", "@The_weapon@说：“把我带到万魔殿！马上！！！”", "@The_weapon@讲述着多头蛇头部数量倍增的有趣故事。", "@The_weapon@说：“有时人们说我是个了不起的混蛋。”", "@The_weapon@咒骂道：“亲爱的日记，今天又是和@player_name@无聊的一天……”", "@The_weapon@说：“根据我的经验，@player_genus_plural@充其量只算得上是平庸。”", "@The_weapon@哼道：“在所有@player_genus_plural@之中，@player_name@是最差的。”", "@The_weapon@说：“放开我，肮脏的恶棍！”", "@The_weapon@沉吟道：“我必须杀掉谁才能在这里获得某些服务？”", "@The_weapon@大声唱道：“高声歌唱，手持大剑！”", "@The_weapon@在思考人和剑在寿命上的差异。", "@The_weapon@说：“只工作不玩耍，@player_name@也会变傻！”", "@The_weapon@说：“我希望我选择了另一个人作为杀戮[工具|傻瓜]。”", "@The_weapon@沙哑地说：“需要喝一杯……快！要刺些什么东西。”", "@The_weapon@说：“你有没有见到我的剑鞘放到了什么地方？我感觉自己太暴露了。”", "@The_weapon@抱怨道：“哦，你的手又湿又油。但我允许你使用我一段时间。”"], "proposed_translation": Merrily, merrily, merrily...\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, \"This blade is made for cutting, and that's just what I'll do...\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, \"We shall overcome...\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, \"Slicing up eyeballs, I want you to know!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, \"So knives out, cook him up, squash his head, put him in the pot.\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, \"Straight outta Zot, crazy bloodletter named Singing Sword.\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, \"Gouge away, you can gouge away...\"", "@The_weapon@ roars, \"The pleasure is to slay, it makes no difference what you say.\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, \"Killing is my business... and business is good!\"", "@The_weapon@ roars, \"I want to gut you like an animal!\"", "@The_weapon@ happily sings, \"...another one bites the dust!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, \"Die when you die!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, \"I will survive!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, \"As long as I know how to carve I know I'm still alive.\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, \"You spin me right round baby, right round...\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"Despicable dogs, put down the jewellery!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"I have faith in your faithlessness, not your fidelity! Pile the hoard... into a heap!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a thundering basso, \"Come here, you cur! If you want to measure, measure yourself against me!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a rumbling basso, \"You slyly... evade the POINT.\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a rumbling basso, \"Shall I exert myself... against the miscreant for you?\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a rumbling basso, \"But fall will the foe... with your cunning's aid!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a rumbling basso, \"Enough, blasphemous fool!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a rumbling basso, \"Leave him... his slavering fury!\""], "current_chinese": ["@The_weapon@唱道：“滚，滚，滚着头，滚动着向前。快活地，快活地，快活地……”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“这个剑刃是被用作切割的，这就是我的用途……”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“我们要战胜一切……”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“割眼球的桥段，我想让你知道！”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“那就掏出刀子，拿他开刀做饭，硬塞他的脑袋，放进煮锅中。”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“冲出佐特领域，一个叫唱歌剑的疯狂放血者。”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“挖出来，你可以挖出来……”", "@The_weapon@咆哮道：“最重要是杀戮的乐趣，所以你说什么也都无所谓。”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“杀戮是哥的生意……生意就是极好的！”", "@The_weapon@咆哮道：“我要像个动物一样掏出你的内脏！”", "@The_weapon@欢快地唱道：“……又干掉一个！”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“该死的时候就去死！”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“我会活下去！”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“只要我还会切割，我就知道我还活着。”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“你让我旋转，宝贝，旋转一圈……”", "@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“无耻之徒，别碰那些宝贝！”", "@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“你的不忠我相信，而非忠诚！但我自信于——能反抗你们全部！”", "@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“对你太难……因为你太蠢！”", "@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“送到那里……听我指令！全部财宝……堆到一起！”", "@The_weapon@用雷鸣般的男低音唱道：“来这，野狗！你想要度量，不如来跟我掂量！”", 1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1]}, "zh_only_variant_ordinals": [], "zh_variant_count": 45}, "current_english": ["@The_weapon@ sings, \"Let it be, let it be...\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, \"What fun it is to ride and sing a slaying song tonight!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, \"Killing me softly with this song...\"", "@The_weapon@ [sings|whistles], \"Always look on the bright side of death...\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, \"This'll be the day that I die...\"", "@The_weapon@ rasps, \"@player_name@, join me in death!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, \"I need a hero!\"", "@The_weapon@ asks plaintively, \"@player_name@, can I go out and kill tonight?\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, \"...and was not in a bit afraid to be killed in nasty ways, many, many nasty ways...\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, \"I am a fighter, not a lover.\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, \"I stabbed a man in the dungeon, just to watch him die.\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a lilting soprano, \"[Heiajaheia! Heiajaheia!|Wagala weia! Wallala, weiala weia!|Wallala! Lalaleia! Leialala!|Heia! Heia! Hahei!]\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a lilting soprano, \"Badly you guard... the sleeping gold; watch better o'er... the slumberer's bed!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"Damp fills my nostrils: curse this sneezing!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"...for hear me, ye waves: thus I curse love!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"Thank you, stupid! Your work has stood the test.\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"The whole world will I win... all for my own!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"All you gods I'll grip... in my golden grasp!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"As I renounced love, all living things... shall love renounce!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"Beware! Beware! For when once you men... serve my might...\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"Beware the nocturnal host... when the Abyssal horde rises... from the silent depths... to the day's light!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"With wisdom the fool... is puffed up to bursting! Now may envy devour you!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"Take heed, haughty god! If I sinned, I sinned only 'gainst myself!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"But you, immortal one, sin against... all that was, is and shall be!\"\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a stentorian basso, \"It shall gladden... no happy man; its bright gleam... shall light on no one lucky!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a thundering basso, \"Many a time have I paid... giants their due.\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a thundering basso, \"A sultry haze... hangs in the air; its dull weight... oppresses me.\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a thundering basso, \"Heda! Heda! Hedo!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a rumbling basso, \"...its glittering glow hides... runes of riches...\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a mocking tenor, \"As you command, I will willingly lead you - steeply down: shall we journey to the Orb?\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a rumbling basso, \"But for all the world... I will not surrender the Orb!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a mocking tenor, \"Then shall we swing down... through the sulphurous cleft?\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a mocking tenor, \"Oh! Oh! Terrible serpent, do not swallow me!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a mocking tenor, \"Slink off home! No knot holds you: go your way freely!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a piercing soprano, \"Woe! Woe is me!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a squeaky tenor, \"Oh, Oh! Ow! Ow! Ow!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a melting tenor, \"How soft the breeze... which breathes on us again!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a mocking tenor, \"Your power lends you... high-flying courage: grim and great... has grown your strength.\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a mocking tenor, \"For moon, stars and the radiant sun... may, too, do no other... than to serve you!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a mocking tenor, \"Take whatever shape you wish... with wonder strike me dumb!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a cavernous basso, \"Ah, her gaze... still gleams toward me; the stars of her eyes... still shine on me!\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a forceful soprano, \"Irredeemably... to dark destruction... its possession dooms you.\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a forceful soprano, \"Hear me! Hear me! Hear me! All that is shall come to an end.\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a forceful soprano, \"A dark day... dawns for the gods.\"", "@The_weapon@ sings, in a forceful soprano, \"Whatever was - I know; whatever is, whatever shall be - I also see.\""], "current_chinese": ["@The_weapon@唱道：“随他去，顺其自然……”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“今晚滑血多快乐，把滑血歌儿唱！”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“这首歌将我温柔处死……”", "@The_weapon@[唱道|吹着口哨]：“总是看着死亡中光明的一面……”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“有朝一日我将逝去……”", "@The_weapon@用刺耳的声音唱道：“@player_name@，与我共长眠吧！”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“我渴望一位英雄！”", "@The_weapon@悲哀地问：“@player_name@，今晚我可以出去杀人吗？”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“……一点也不怕被残忍地杀死，许多许多残忍的方式……”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“我是个战士，而不是个爱人。”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“我在地牢杀了人，并且眼睁睁的看着他断气。”", "@The_weapon@用轻快的女高音唱道：“[嗨啊呀嗨啊！嗨啊呀嗨啊！|哇啦啦嗨啊！哇啦啦，哇啊啦嗨啊！|哇啦啦！啦啦哎呀！哎呀啦哎！|嗨啊！嗨啊！哈嘿！]”", "@The_weapon@用轻快的女高音唱道：“这黄金的沉睡，你们守得真糟……要更好地看护它沉睡的温床！”", "@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“潮湿填满了我的鼻子：该死的喷嚏！”", "@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“……听好了，波浪，我如此诅咒这爱！”", "@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“谢谢你，蠢货！你的活干得不错。”", "@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“把整个世界……据为自己所有！”", "@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“用黄金之拳，我会攥住……你们所有的神！”", "@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“就像我把爱拒绝，一切的生灵……该把它弃绝！”", "@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“留神！留神！因为当你们男人……服从我之力……”", "@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“留神！留神这暗夜的大军……升起那深渊的宝藏……从死寂之底到日下！”", "@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“一点小聪明……就胀得要炸的蠢货！你分明是嫉恨！”", "@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“当心了，高高在上的神！我若有罪，这罪也只对我自己！”", "@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“但对那一切过去，现在和将来，有罪的，是……你！”", "@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“没有愉者……该为它欢愉，没人有幸……得到它光辉的笑！”", "@The_weapon@用雷鸣般的男低音唱道：“付……巨人报酬，我可是常客。”", "@The_weapon@用雷鸣般的男低音唱道：“闷热的水汽……悬在空中；我恼于……这沉闷压迫！”", "@The_weapon@用雷鸣般的男低音唱道：“嘿哒！嘿哒！嘿哆！”", "@The_weapon@用低沉的男低音唱道：“劫掠的符文藏在……它的闪光之中……”", "@The_weapon@用嘲弄的男高音唱道：“随你的命令，我乐意带领你——我们深入地底：可要寻求宝珠？”", "@The_weapon@用低沉的男低音唱道：“但为全世界……我都不会交出宝珠！”", "@The_weapon@用嘲弄的男高音唱道：“那我们扭着……穿过硫磺谷？”", "@The_weapon@用嘲弄的男高音唱道：“哦唉！哦唉！可怕的恶龙，别吞了我！”", "@The_weapon@用嘲弄的男高音唱道：“溜回家吧！没绳子绑着你了，随便走吧！”", "@The_weapon@用尖厉的女高音唱道：“悲啊！悲啊！”", "@The_weapon@用短促的男高音唱道：“哦唉！哦唉！嗷！嗷！嗷！”", "@The_weapon@用柔和的男高音唱道：“多可爱的风……又为我们吹起！”", "@The_weapon@用嘲弄的男高音唱道：“通天的胆子……赋自你的魔法，恐怖地……增长着你的力量。”", "@The_weapon@用嘲弄的男高音唱道：“因为明月星辰，和那光辉的太阳……它们也不敢做别的……只能去服务你！”", "@The_weapon@用嘲弄的男高音唱道：“随你所愿……只要能惊愕我这蠢物！”", "@The_weapon@用空洞的低音唱道：“唉呀，她的目光……仍闪烁着；她眼里的星……仍照耀着我！”", "@The_weapon@用强有力的女高音唱道：“绝境般……黑暗的灾厄……于你它注定。”", "@The_weapon@用强有力的女高音唱道：“听着！听着！听着！一切的如今，终结。”", "@The_weapon@用强有力的女高音唱道：“一个昏晦之日……昏临着众神。”", "@The_weapon@用强有力的女高音唱道：“一切何往——知于我；一切何为，一切将何为——也见于我。”"], "proposed_translation": ["@The_weapon@唱道：“随他去，顺其自然……”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“今晚滑血多快乐，把滑血歌儿唱！”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“这首歌将我温柔处死……”", "@The_weapon@[唱道|吹着口哨]：“总是看着死亡中光明的一面……”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“有朝一日我将逝去……”", "@The_weapon@用刺耳的声音唱道：“@player_name@，与我共长眠吧！”", "@The_weapon@唱道：“我渴望一位英雄！”", "@The_weapon@用轻快的女高音唱道：“[嗨啊呀嗨啊！嗨啊呀嗨啊！|哇啦啦嗨啊！哇啦啦，哇啊啦嗨啊！|哇啦啦！啦啦哎呀！哎呀啦哎！|嗨啊！嗨啊！哈嘿！]”", "@The_weapon@用轻快的女高音唱道：“这黄金的沉睡，你们守得真糟……要更好地看护它沉睡的温床！”", "@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“潮湿填满了我的鼻子：该死的喷嚏！”", "@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“……听好了，波浪，我如此诅咒这爱！”", "@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“谢谢你，蠢货！你的活干得不错。”", "@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“把整个世界……据为自己所有！”", "@The_weapon@用洪亮的男低音唱道：“用黄金之拳，我会攥住……你们所有的神！”", "reviewer_rationale": "批次 A。19 个现行变体：ordinal 18 为 definite fix（重复的 的 + balm 香脂）；其余 keep。EN-only ordinals 19/20（兽人金矿、我们曾欢笑）为真缺失变体，已人工确认并随 1de9250b 落地（v2 reviewed_actions add@19/add@20，绑定候选序数 19/20 的精确文本）。", "rejected_alternatives": ["保留 可爱的的（重复的）", "保留 芳香（balm 为药草香脂）", "不补齐 EN-only 19/20（2 条缺失变体提议：@The_weapon@唱道：“兽人得了金矿，我得的却是矿井……” / @The_weapon@唱道：“我们曾欢笑，我们曾快乐，我们在阳光下斩杀恶魔……”）"], "reviewed_actions": [{"kind": "add", "variant_ordinal": 19, "text": "@The_weapon@唱道：“兽人得了金矿，我得的却是矿井……”", "rationale": "基线 EN[19]（Orcs got the gold mine, I got the shaft...）为真缺失变体（批次 A 确认，非位置伪影）；经人工确认批准补齐，候选序数 19 即本译文。协议与 EN[19] 逐项一致：无控制前缀、runtime token @The_weapon@、无随机子串站、无 Lua 站/比较串。"}, {"kind": "add", "variant_ordinal": 20, "text": "@The_weapon@唱道：“我们曾欢笑，我们曾快乐，我们在阳光下斩杀恶魔……”", "rationale": "基线 EN[20]（We had joy, we had fun, we slew demons in the sun...）为真缺失变体（批次 A 确认）；经人工确认批准补齐，候选序数 20 即本译文。协议与 EN[20] 逐项一致：无控制前缀、runtime token @The_weapon@、无随机子串站、无 Lua 站/比较串。"}], Die. Die!\"", "@The_weapon@ shouts, \"You shall lie beneath the ground but I, I will glint in the sun!\"", "@The_weapon@ shouts, \"Born from this womb, sliced by this blade!\"", "@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"When this war is won, I will wage the next one.\"", "@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"Blade-deep in blood!\"", "@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"I revel, I sever!\"", "@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"All those pillows!\"", "@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"No rest until they move no more!\"", "@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"As long as they die, we live!\"", "@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"The future is bloody!\"", "@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"The bloody future is ours!\"", "@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"Dismember the living, dismember the dead!\"", "@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"We will celebrate on a pile of carcasses!\"", "@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"@player_name@, you will [dine|feast] on fine @_corpses_@ today!\"", "@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"Go for the jugular!\"", "@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"My instinct to kill is stronger than their will to live!\"", "@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"I am covered in blood! It feels so good!\"", "@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"Aah! This piece of @_body_part_@ is stuck to me. Wipe it off!\"", "@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"My edge gets dull! My edge gets dull!\"", "@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"Don't stick me into that one, please.\"", "@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"My victories add up!\"", "@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"Blood as far as the eye can see! Blood for all eternity!\"", "@The_weapon@ @_screams_@, \"Blood must spurt and blood must flow!
